使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only net whose “play‐in‐pentagon” face has the circle–X above it and the star to its right (just as on the folded cube) is choice A. So the correct answer is A.

预测答案: D

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only net in which the “heavy” (thick‐lined) pentagon has the star face glued to its bottom edge, the thinner pentagon glued to its top, and the circle‐with‐diamond glued to its right is choice D. None of the others can match all three adjacencies simultaneously. So the correct answer is D.

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The net in C is the only one in which the “circle‐around‐square” face has exactly the “gear” face attached on one side (which becomes the top) and the “triangle‐in‐pentagon” face on the other (which becomes the right) so that, when folded, you reproduce the three thick‐lined faces shown in the 3D drawing. None of the other nets lines up those three patterns in the correct adjacencies.  

Answer: C.

题目: _3DTo2D_N/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_6-answ_D.png
Prompt类型: N类型
正确答案: D
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-24 01:34:22
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Here is the reasoning using the 5‐across net (with the middle square as the “front” face, one above it as “top,” one below as “bottom,” and the two to its left/right as “left/right”).

1. From the folded cube drawing, the three visible faces are:
   – Front face = the thick hexagon with the spiky (“star”) interior   
   – Top face   = the plain 5-sided pentagon  
   – Right face = the hexagon with the small square interior  

2. On the net (middle of the 5 across), we therefore need:
   – Center (front) square  = hexagon★ (“star” inside)  
   – Square above center    = pentagon  
   – Square to the right     = hexagon■ (“square” inside)  

3. Scanning the four candidate nets A–D, only option D places:
   – A ★-hexagon in the exact middle  
   – A pentagon directly above it  
   – A □-hexagon immediately to its right  

Hence the correct match is D.
==================================================

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only net that has the large circle‐in‐diamond cell flanked on one edge by the thick “square‐frame” cell (which becomes the top) and on the adjacent edge by the tall, overlapping‐diamonds cell (which becomes the right), and in its two lower positions the small “diamond‐in‐square” and the rotated square‐with‐circle cells exactly where the bottom and back go, is option D.    

So the correct choice is D.

题目: _3DTo2D_N/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_36-answ_B.png
Prompt类型: N类型
正确答案: B
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-24 02:38:38
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Here’s the quick way to see it on the nets:

1. On the assembled cube (top diagram) the three *bold* faces are  
   – Front: the big diamond‐with‐circle motif  
   – Top: the plain square‐border  
   – Right: the circle‐in‐square with cross‐lines  

2. In each candidate net A–D, find the diamond‐face and check which two faces in that net actually end up *sharing edges* with it when you fold up the cube.  
   – Only in choice D do those two neighbors (in the flat net) fold up to become exactly the square‐border on top and the circle‐in‐square on the right.  

Hence the correct unfolded‐net is option D.  

Answer: D
==================================================

So the correct answer is B.

题目: _3DTo2D_N/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_56-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: N类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: B
时间: 2025-07-24 09:17:04
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: B

Explanation (mapping net B’s faces to the cube):

• In net B, the face one square above the “hinge” face (the one at row 1, column 3) is the nested-pentagon/star motif – this folds up to become the cube’s top face, exactly as shown.  
• The hinge face itself (row 2, column 3) in net B carries the three-triangle/pentagon motif – this folds forward to form the cube’s front face.  
• Immediately to the right of that (row 2, column 4) is the grid/spikey-inside-pentagon motif – this becomes the cube’s right face.  
• To the left (row 2, column 2) is the sun-burst motif – this becomes the left face (faintly visible through the wireframe).  
• One square directly below the hinge face (row 3, column 3) is the smaller nested-pentagon motif – this becomes the cube’s bottom.  
• Finally, the face two to the right (row 2, column 5) is the final spiky-sun/star motif – this becomes the back face (seen faintly through the cube).

All six patterns from net B line up perfectly with the 3D preview.
==================================================

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only unfolding whose arm‐of‐2 and arm‐of‐4 sit in the right places (triangle‐in‐circle above the 2nd square and the “arrow” triangle below the 4th), and whose left‐to‐right row of four faces reads (diamond–gear–double-diamond–hexagon), is choice A. So the correct net is A.

题目: _3DTo2D_N/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_137-answ_C.png
Prompt类型: N类型
正确答案: C
预测答案: A
时间: 2025-07-24 11:49:40
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Here’s a quick way to see why only A can work:

1. In the folded cube (the semi‐transparent 3D drawing up top) the “solid” blue pentagon face (let’s call it Q) is surrounded on all four sides by exactly four other faces (the narrow pentagon P, the hollow pentagon R, the thin‐outline pentagon T and the concentric‐oval face U).  On a cube‐net, the only cell that has four neighbours (N, E, S, W) is the centre cell of the “T-shaped” net (i.e. row 2, column 2 in the little 3×3 array).  Hence in the correct net Q must sit in that middle cell.

2. In A you can see the large filled pentagon is exactly in the middle cell of the T–net, with the other four (P, R, T, U) abutting it on its four sides, and the only remaining face (the square + triangle, S) dangling off to one side (which becomes the hidden back face when you fold up the cube).

3. In B, C or D the large filled pentagon is never in that central “four‐neighbour” spot, so you can’t get all four of P, R, T, U adjacent to it when folded.

Therefore the one true matching net is option A.
==================================================

大模型完整回复:

None of B, C or D ever has a cell of degree four, so they can’t produce the correct folded cube.  Hence the correct choice is A.
==================================================

题目: _3DTo2D_N/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_18-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: N类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-24 12:47:05
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Explanation:

Label the six faces by the six distinct symbols you see on the folded cube:

  1. A filled‐in pentagon inside a circle  
  2. An unfilled (outline‐only) pentagon inside a circle  
  3. A “hash” (cross‐hatched) motif inside an oval  
  4. The same hash motif (appearing twice on the cube)  
  5. A parallelogram‐grid pattern  
  6. A diamond‐border outline  

On the folded cube (top illustration) the filled‐pentagon face is adjacent on its:
  – top edge to a hash‐in‐oval face  
  – bottom edge to the parallelogram‐grid face  
  – left edge to the diamond‐border face  
  – right edge to the outline‐pentagon face  

Of the four nets, only net D places exactly those six symbols into a cube‐net so that the filled pentagon in the net has exactly those four neighbors.  In particular, in net D:

  • The filled Pentagon–in–Circle square sits immediately to the right of the top‐row “hash in oval” square.  
  • Directly below it in the net is the parallelogram‐grid square.  
  • Directly to its left is the diamond‐border square.  
  • And directly to its right is the outline‐only Pentagon–in–Circle square.

Folded up, that matches the adjacency relationships shown in the 3D cube. No other net (A, B or C) places the six distinct symbols with exactly those adjacencies.
==================================================

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Let the front‐face “X‐in‐square’’ be F.  On the assembled cube F’s four neighbors are

 • the double-quadrilateral face (the top),  
 • the elongated diamond (the right),  
 • the parallelogram-double-border (the bottom),  
 • the pentagon (the left, shown faintly).

Only net C places exactly those four around the X‐face in one central cell with the remaining two faces in the correct positions (one above, one below).  Neither A nor D gives F four net‐adjacencies, and in B one of F’s neighbors is wrong.  Hence the correct choice is C.

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Explanation (concise):

Among the four candidate nets, only option D places the six faces in exactly the same adjacency relationships as those on the folded cube (the ring-face above the front-face, the diamond-face to its right, the star-face to its left, the elongated-hexagon beneath it, and the circle-X on the one remaining flap). Options A and B do not give the front-face four neighbors, and options B and C mis‐place the diamond or star relative to the front. Net D is the only one whose “plus”‐shaped layout of faces folds up to reproduce the same pattern arrangement shown on the cube.

Explanation:

Label the six faces of the cube-net by their pattern and by their net‐coordinates (x,y), taking the “row of four” through the circle/triangle face as y=0:

  – (–1,0): the diamond-with‐triangle  
  – ( 0,0): the circle-with‐triangle (the “play” symbol)  
  – ( 1,0): the pentagon‐outline with interior star  
  – ( 2,0): the small diamond‐outline with interior square  
  – ( 0,1): the seven‐point star  
  – ( 0,–1): the square‐with‐spikes  

Only net B places exactly those six patterns in exactly those relative positions. None of the other three nets (A, C or D) puts the central circle‐triangle at (0,0) flanked by the diamond on the left and pentagon on the right, with the seven‐point star above and the spiky square below.
==================================================

题目: _3DTo2D_N/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_139-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: N类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-24 17:36:00
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Explanation:

1. In the folded cube, the “×‐inside‐circle” face (the front) touches exactly four other faces: the ring, the arrow, the star, and the nested-pentagon.  In the net, that means the square with the “×‐circle” must have neighbors on all four sides.

2. In options A, B, and C the “×‐circle” square in the net has fewer than four adjacent squares.  Only in D does the “×‐circle” have one square on its left, one on its right, one above, and one below, just as on the cube.

3. A quick check of the labels around the “×‐circle” in D shows exactly the correct four patterns in the correct relative positions, so D is the only net that folds to the given cube.

Therefore the correct choice is D.
==================================================

题目: _3DTo2D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_2-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: A
时间: 2025-07-24 17:56:09
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: A

Explanation:

1. On the folded cube (top diagram), the face showing the large diamond‐with‐triangle motif is the front face, and the face showing the solid (filled) hexagon is its opposite (the back face).  
2. Therefore in any valid net these two faces must not share an edge.  
3. In net A, however, the filled hexagon square is placed directly adjacent (sharing an edge) to the diamond‐triangle square, which would fold them to adjacent faces rather than opposite faces.  
4. Nets B, C and D all place the filled hexagon and the diamond‐triangle faces nonadjacent (so they can fold to opposites), but net A does not.  

Hence A does NOT match the folded cube.
==================================================

Hence the one that does NOT match is:

A

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only net in which “opposite‐face” pairs (circle vs. gear, the two hexagon‐ring patterns, and the two diamond patterns) end up adjacent to each other is B.  In all the others those three forbidden adjacencies are avoided.  Hence B cannot fold into the given cube.  

Answer: B

Hence A cannot match the folded cube.

题目: _3DTo2D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_83-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: B
时间: 2025-07-24 19:57:51
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only one of the four nets that actually reproduces the cube’s true opposite‐face relationships (1↔4, 2↔6, 3↔5) is B.  A, C and D all put a pair of opposite faces next to each other in the net, so they cannot fold up correctly.  Hence the net that **does** match the cube is B – equivalently, that means B is the only one that *doesn’t* violate the cube’s layout.  So the answer is B.
==================================================

==================================================
题目: _3DTo2D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_84-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: C
时间: 2025-07-24 20:00:43
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Let F1–F4 be the four “equator” faces in the net (row 2, col 1–4), F5 the one “up” (row 1,col 1) and F6 the one “down” (row 3,col 4).  When folded so that

– F1 becomes the front (shows the thick‐line pentagon–square),  
– F2 becomes the right (shows the rhombus),  
– F5 becomes the top,  
– F6 becomes the bottom (the small triangle‐in‐hexagon),  

only choice C fails to put the pentagon–square in F1 and the rhombus in F2.  All the other nets can be folded so F1 is the thick pentagon–square and F2 the diamond, but C cannot.  Hence C is the only one that does *not* match the folded cube.  

Answer: C.
==================================================

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only net that fails to put the pentagon (front) immediately “under” the sun (top) is B.  In B the sun (at (1,2)) is neighbored only by two diamonds, not by the pentagon, so you can’t fold B up to get the sun–pentagon–diamond triplet shown on the completed cube.  A, C and D all place the pentagon directly adjacent to the sun on the right edge, just as on the real cube.  Hence B is the one that does *not* match.

预测答案: B
时间: 2025-07-24 20:21:26
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
To rule out the “impostor” net you only have to note that the filled‐pentagon motif (the “thick” pentagon with a solid centre) sits on the front face of the real cube, so it can never appear on any other square in a correct net.  In choice B that filled‐pentagon is shown on the bottom flap, so B cannot fold up to give the cube you were shown.  Hence B is the one that does *not* match.  

Answer: B

题目: _3DTo2D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_126-answ_A.png

预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-24 21:15:47
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Brief explanation: In the original net the six faces lie in a “zig-zag” chain (only the two ends have just one neighbor), so whichever candidate net fails to reproduce that same 1–2–2–2–1 adjacency pattern must be the wrong one.  Options A, B and C all show exactly two faces touching in the middle of the chain and only one at each end, just as in the given net.  Option D, however, arranges its six faces in a full 3×2 block, so it does not match the original face-adjacencies and therefore cannot fold to the same cube.
==================================================

题目: _3DTo2D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_131-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: A
时间: 2025-07-24 21:22:40
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: A

Explanation (concise):  
Label the six faces by their shapes:
- F0 = bold pentagon  
- F1 = diamond  
- F2 = circle/oval  
- F3 = triangle  
- F4 = slender pentagon  
- F5 = hexagon  

From the net we have the opposite‐face pairs  
(F0–F5), (F1–F3), (F2–F4).  
Any valid folding net must never place an opposite pair adjacent.

In option A’s top row the slender pentagon (F4) sits immediately next to the circle/oval (F2), but F2 and F4 are opposite faces in the original net. Hence A cannot fold to the same cube, while B, C, and D respect all opposite pair separations.
==================================================

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only net that can’t possibly fold up to give the three “ring”‐faces (star‐in‐a‐ring, hexagon‐in‐a‐ring and heptagon‐in‐a‐ring) all adjacent exactly as on the cube is A. 

Answer: A

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The net shown in option B cannot possibly fold up to give the front‐face (square‐in‐pentagon) as seen on the cube—no matter how you rotate or flip it, you never get the square‐inside‐pentagon in the “front” position. Hence B does NOT match.

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only net that fails to place the “star–pentagon” face in the middle (so that it can have four neighbours) is D – in all the other nets you can find exactly one square with four adjacent faces, but in D no face sits in the “central” spot. Hence D cannot fold up to the given cube. Answer: D.

题目: _3DTo2D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_144-answ_C.png

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only arrangement violating the fact that the “circle‐in‐a‐diamond” face (which is opposite the big hexagon/star in the original net) must never share an edge with it is choice C. All the others respect that opposite‐face relationship, but in C the circle/diamond and the hexagon/star end up adjacent. Hence C cannot fold up to give the same cube.

题目: _3DTo2D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_12-answ_D.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: D
预测答案: C
时间: 2025-07-24 22:01:44
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only net that cannot possibly fold up to give the three faces shown (Double‐Diamond+Triangle front, Circle+Border‐Diamond right, Circle+Filled‐Diamond top) is C. In every other layout those three patterns can be made to become front, right and top by an appropriate folding (and rotation) of the net – in C there simply is no way to get both the filled‐diamond‐in‐circle and the border‐diamond‐in‐circle into the correct adjacency around the double‐diamond‐in‐triangle face. So the one that does *not* match is C.

题目: _3DTo2D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_15-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: A
时间: 2025-07-24 22:09:13
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The quickest way is to look at which “net” puts the circle-in-diamond face (the one we know is on the right of the #-square front) next to the #-square in the flat layout.  Only in choice A do those two end up side-by-side (i.e. adjacent) in the net – but on the real cube they are on opposite sides of the front, not next to it.  Hence A cannot possibly fold up to the given cube.  

Answer: A

题目: _3DTo2D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_29-answ_C.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: C
预测答案: A
时间: 2025-07-24 22:27:10
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: A

Explanation:
From the original net we can read off which faces are opposite and which share an edge:

– The “star-burst” (front) face is surrounded on four sides by
   • the small “#” grid,
   • the elongated hexagon,
   • the diamond-in-square, and
   • the circle-with-X.
  Opposite it (i.e. not edge-adjacent) is the play-triangle circle.

– The “#” grid and the elongated hexagon are thus opposite each other.
– The diamond-square and the circle-with-X are opposite each other.
– The front-star is opposite the play-circle.

In option A, however, the play-circle is placed adjacent to the star-burst (and the elongated hexagon never appears at all).  This contradicts both the required opposite-face relationship (play vs. star) and the requirement that the elongated hexagon appear exactly once.  Hence A cannot fold up to the given cube.
==================================================

大模型完整回复:

All of the other nets (A, C, and D) keep the hexagon and square‐diamond faces nonadjacent, so only B fails to match.

Reasoning: In the original net the hexagon face (on top) is only edge‐adjacent to the triangle (the right face) along its right side. In option C the triangle is attached to the bottom edge of the hexagon, which would make it the front (or back) face instead – but the front face is the big square, not the triangle. Therefore C cannot be folded into the same cube.

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only net in which two faces that end up opposite on the cube are drawn edge‐adjacent is (C).  In (C) the “big‐diamond” and the “pentagon” faces touch in the net, but on the folded cube they must lie opposite one another, so (C) cannot be the correct unfolding.  Hence the one that does NOT match is C.

预测答案: A

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: A

Explanation:

If you inspect each of the four “maps” of the six faces (A–D) as an adjacency diagram (center face plus its four side‐neighbors plus the opposite face), you find that in A no face ever has all four of its neighbors (up, down, left, right) attached to it.  In a valid cube‐face adjacency diagram exactly one cell (the front face) must border four others; in A no cell has four neighbors, so A cannot possibly fold up to the given cube.  B, C and D each do contain one cell with exactly four neighbors, so only A fails to match.

题目: _3DTo2D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_131-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: C
时间: 2025-07-25 01:11:04
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: C

Explanation (sketch):  
Label the six faces of the cube in the original unfolded pattern as follows:

  • Face 1 = the face bearing the concentric rings  
  • Face 2 = the face bearing the “star‐in‐circle” motif  
  • Face 3 = the face bearing the simple pentagon outline  
  • Face 4 = the face bearing the pentagon with inner pentagon  
  • Face 5 = the face bearing the pentagon with “X” inside  
  • Face 6 = the face bearing the “X‐in‐circle” motif  

In the given unfolding, the adjacency relations are:

  – Face 1 is adjacent to Faces 2 (to its left), 3 (above), 5 (to its right), and 6 (below).  
  – Faces 2 & 5 are opposites (they never touch each other).  
  – Faces 3 & 6 are opposites.  
  – Faces 4 & 1 are opposites.

You can check each of the four candidate nets A–D against those adjacency/opposition requirements.  Net C is the only one whose placement forces two faces that must be opposite (in the original cube) to become adjacent instead.  All of A, B and D respect the required adjacencies and can fold to the same cube, but C cannot. Therefore C is the one that does *not* match.
==================================================

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only “net’’ that has the wrong adjacency cannot be D.  A 2×3 block of faces (as in D) cannot be re‐arranged to match the given cube’s T–shaped net, so D is the impossible one.  

Answer: D

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only net that places the pure‐circle face immediately next to the three‐nested–hexagons face (they’re actually opposite each other on the real cube) is choice C. All the others respect the actual face‐opposition relationships, so C cannot fold up to give the cube shown.

题目: _3DTo2D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_149-answ_B.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: B
预测答案: C
时间: 2025-07-25 01:43:32
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The one net that fails to put the “circle‐rings” face next to the “nested‐hexagon/diamond” (the original front face) is C. In C they only meet at a corner, not along an edge, so C cannot fold to the given cube. X = C.
